### Subsystem Registry Design Notes

The 10-subsystem layout reflects two first-class groups:

- **SS-01 through SS-04** are **Subsystem A** (Rust compiled artifacts). They share one Cargo workspace, one release binary per platform, and the WASM plugin ABI contract.
- **SS-05 through SS-10** are **Subsystem B** (VSDD orchestration framework). They share one plugin manifest, one marketplace entry, and the Lobster workflow format.

**Deviations from the suggested 10-subsystem template:**

- SS-05 was renamed from "Pipeline Orchestration" to "Pipeline Orchestration" — kept, but agents were moved in (34 agents belong here alongside workflows because agents are the steps' executors; splitting them would orphan the orchestrator).
- SS-06 "Skill Catalog" is the largest single BC surface (585 BCs as of v1.0.0-beta.6; see Subsystem Registry table for current count). It is intentionally a standalone subsystem because skills have independent behavioral contracts per skill (each SKILL.md is a discrete unit of behavior).
- SS-09 "Configuration and Activation" is narrower than the suggested name — it covers only the activation plumbing and CI variant generation, not general config. `hooks-registry.toml` routing lives in SS-07 (Hook Bash Layer) because it is the routing table for that layer.
- SS-10 merges "CLI Tools" and "Bin" because all 12 bin tools are CLI-invocable and the commands/ slash-command bindings are just thin wrappers around skills.

## Component Dependency Map

```mermaid
graph TD
    CC["Claude Code (harness)"]
    SS09["SS-09: Configuration and Activation"]
    SS01["SS-01: Hook Dispatcher Core"]
    SS02["SS-02: Hook SDK and Plugin ABI"]
    SS03["SS-03: Event Emission (OTel-Aligned)"]
    SS04["SS-04: Plugin Ecosystem"]
    SS05["SS-05: Pipeline Orchestration"]
    SS06["SS-06: Skill Catalog"]
    SS07["SS-07: Hook Bash Layer"]
    SS08["SS-08: Templates and Rules"]
    SS10["SS-10: CLI Tools and Bin"]

    CC -->|"reads hooks.json"| SS09
    SS09 -->|"generated variants wire to"| SS01
    SS01 -->|"loads + executes WASM via"| SS02
    SS01 -->|"writes all events to (single-stream)"| SS03
    SS01 -->|"invokes plugins from"| SS04
    SS04 -->|"shells out via exec_subprocess to"| SS07
    SS05 -->|"dispatches sub-agents who call"| SS06
    SS05 -->|"gate writes via"| SS07
    SS06 -->|"renders output from"| SS08
    SS06 -->|"emits events via"| SS10
    SS10 -->|"reads/writes state in"| SS05
```

**Strict dependency direction:** data flows down (CC → SS09 → SS01 → SS02/SS03/SS04 → SS07). The orchestration stack (SS-05/06/07/08/10) is a separate plane. SS-07 sits at the intersection: bash hooks are invoked by both SS-04 (via legacy-bash-adapter) and SS-05/SS-06 (as gates on tool use).

## Cross-Cutting Concerns

| Concern | Owner | Mechanism |
|---------|-------|-----------|
| Observability (single-stream) | SS-03 | `events-YYYY-MM-DD.jsonl` — all lifecycle + domain events (ADR-015). Debug file `dispatcher-internal-*.jsonl` opt-in via `VSDD_DEBUG_LOG=1`; ADR-007 amended. |
| Capability enforcement | SS-01 | Deny-by-default; cap-gated host fns emit denial event + return code |
| Schema versioning | SS-01, SS-09, SS-03 | per-config: `hooks-registry.toml` schema_version=2 (post-ADR-019; v1→v2 hard-error, no compat shim); `observability-config.toml` schema_version=2 (post-ADR-015 D-15.1; v1→v2 hard-errors with migration hint per BC-3.05.004 PC4); other TOML configs schema_version=1; mismatch = hard error per DI-014 |
| Trace correlation | SS-01 | `trace_id` (UUID v4) propagated on every emitted event (renamed from `dispatcher_trace_id` per DI-017 / ADR-015 v1.7) |
| Platform selection | SS-09 | Activation skill copies `hooks.json.<platform>` (ADR-009) |
| Error non-blocking | SS-01 | Registry/payload/engine errors → `internal.dispatcher_error` → exit 0 |
| Bash hook compatibility | SS-04 | `legacy-bash-adapter.wasm` via `exec_subprocess` (ADR-012) |
| Secrets protection | SS-07 | `protect-secrets.sh` PreToolUse gate + env_allow deny-by-default |

| ADR-024 | Dispatcher log-dir worktree-aware resolution, CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT fail-loud contract, resolver WASM plugin path resolution, internal-error dedup, and destructive-guard shadow exception — 7-level resolution order (VSDD_LOG_DIR override → FACTORY_ROOT override → basename-is-.factory guard → walk-up ancestor → cwd-child .factory dir exists → git-worktree-main-root subprocess [200ms timeout] → cwd fallback); fail-loud-but-continue on absent CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT; per-session dedup of `internal.dispatcher_error` (fixed-cap HashSet<u64>, cap 1024, bounded raw Value::as_str() at 4096-byte char-safe ceiling per v1.2 amendment); applies to `load_registry` + single production `get_or_compile` call site; Decision 5 — `dispatcher.started` MUST include `log_dir` from InternalLog::log_dir(); v1.4 (S-18.14 pass-1 adversary fix burst): §Decision 5 §Purity Boundary phantom `InternalLog::write_started` reference corrected to actual emission anchors; F-5 SS-04 advisory resolved NO); AMENDED v1.5 2026-06-22 (S-18.14 pass-2 adversary fix burst: F-1 single call site corrected + BC-1.01.004 precedent cross-reference); **AMENDED v1.6 2026-06-22 (S-18.14 pass-4 adversary fix burst: F-2 §Files-to-change resolver_loader.rs row stale plural 'at all call sites' swept — reworded to single-production-call-site framing; | ADR-030 | pr-manager merge-operation integrity enforcement — three-component mechanical enforcement for D-749 (stale-HEAD READY verdict race) and D-750 (release-PR squash-merge not mechanically enforced): (1) `pr-manager-completion-guard.wasm` SubagentStop advisory gate — READY verdict lacking `covered_sha` emits `READY_SHA_MISSING` advisory (`on_error="continue"` advisory-block-mode via stdout outcome; priority=920); (2) `bin/check-stale-verdict.sh` — compares `covered_sha` against live `gh pr view --json headRefOid`; exits STALE_READY_VERDICT on drift; orchestrator MUST invoke before any READY-verdict merge; fail-closed on gh failure; (3) `bin/enforce-merge-strategy.sh` — wraps `gh pr merge`; forces `--merge` for `^release/v` branches; exits RELEASE_PR_SQUASH_FORBIDDEN on `--squash`/`--rebase` attempt; (4) `release-branch-guardrail.yml` adjudicated NOT extendable (`pull_request` events carry no `merge_method` field); **Alternatives rejected:** Option B blocking SubagentStop stale-check rejected (broad `binary_allow` + fail-closed on gh auth destroys pr-manager output); Option C bash hook command-content match rejected (fragile); Option D prompt-only rejected per F-P2-002 (LLM prompt-spec unenforceable by bats); normative twin BC-5.42.001; CAP-033; rationale roots D-749+D-750+F-P2-002 — **ACCEPTED 2026-07-06; ADR-030 v1.0; E-19 adv-P3 F-P3-015 close-out; SS-04 (WASM plugin crate) + SS-05 (pr-manager agent domain) + SS-07 (hooks-registry SubagentStop entry) + SS-10 (bin tools); v1.1 2026-07-08 D-775: F-W1V-001..004 adjudications — bin/ path confirmed (orchestrator-invoked SS-10 CLI, precedent factory-lock-write.sh/factory-cas-push.sh); positional signatures adopted (spec-wins; T-003/T-004 assert); READY_SHA_FETCH_FAILED + READY_SHA_MISSING named codes adopted; CHECK_STALE_VERDICT_ERROR retained for EC-003 + unclassified arms; exit 1 adopted (S-19.01 AC-002/RG-003); v1.2 2026-07-08 D-776: F-P22-003 Decision 1 TOML tool filter ^Agent → ^Agent$ (fully-anchored singleton form per S-19.04 D-f convention; prevents substring match on AgentX-style tool names); v1.3 2026-07-08 D-777: F-P23-001 canonical TOML descriptor rewritten to live-registry shape — on_error="continue" advisory-block-mode via stdout outcome; priority=920; tool=/tier= fields REMOVED (not present in live SubagentStop entries); F-P22-003 superseded (^Agent$ was anchoring a field that doesn't exist); v1.4 2026-07-11 D-829: §D3 governed-pass-through wrapper contract (arch-wrapper 447bff71; S-19.01 F-P7-001)** | SS-04, SS-05, SS-07, SS-10 | decisions/ADR-030-pr-manager-merge-operation-integrity-enforcement.md |

S-21.01) + skill-doc merge pre-check BC-5.43.001 (Layer 2: git diff --name-only HEAD..<target> halts on .factory/ path); (2) crate naming MANDATORY: validate-factory-path-staging NOT validate-artifact-path (existing validate-artifact-path.wasm is Edit/Write/MultiEdit guard for BC-4.11.001 — functionally disjoint; not documented in any single agent protocol file; server-side origination is the primary Layer-2 threat vector: contributor PR adding .factory/-pathed files merged server-side bypasses Layer-1 entirely, next git pull delivers tracked content); | ADR-032 | verify-state-timestamp-refresh — Edit/MultiEdit payload-targeted timestamp enforcement: Decision 1 — for Edit/MultiEdit, scan new_string values for top-level `timestamp:` OR `factory_lock:` fields; if neither is set in any new_string (payload-neutral — neither field), return `Continue` after skipping the timestamp and lock-expiry checks (Steps 4–7; AC-020 sentinel emitted); Decision 2 — Write tool path unchanged (full Steps 4–8 apply unconditionally); Decision 3 — lock-expiry check (Step 7) fires when `sets_timestamp==true` (always, regardless of `sets_factory_lock` — enforces PC4: timestamp-advancing Edit must also renew `factory_lock.expires_at` in same payload; Block(LockExpiryStale) if stale) OR when `sets_factory_lock==true` without `sets_timestamp` (Step 7 only; skip Steps 4–6); payload-neutral Edits skip Steps 4–7; Step 7 wrapped in `if sets_factory_lock \|\| sets_timestamp`; Decision 4 — new `pub fn new_string_sets_field(new_string: &str, field_key: &str) -> bool` helper (NEW function; 0 matches in crates/ pre-implementation); Decision 5 — AC-020 added to guard_logic function docstring `# BC traces` list. Second deliverable: AC-021 PostToolUse advisory sentinel per Bash git-commit events on factory-artifacts worktree; HEAD vs HEAD^ STATE.md `timestamp:` comparison via dispatcher-injected payload.extra['git_context'] fields (exec-free WASM; ADR-032-AC021-prereq; `on_error=continue`; no sidecar file). 11 tests (4 Red Gates + 7 regression guards). D-866 root-cause; Implementer Work Spec included.
